'0')}`;

```
import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  FlatList,
  Pressable,
  Text,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useFocusEffect, useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { ArrowRight, CheckCircle2, RefreshCw, Shield, Smartphone, Sms } from 'lucide-react-native';

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/alert-dialog';

import {
  discoverSmsSources,
  requestSmsPermission,
  verifyProviderSource,
  type DiscoveryResult,
} from '@/services/providerSourceService';
import type { DiscoveredSmsSource, ProviderName } from '@/types/provider';

const providerLabels: Record<ProviderName, string> = {
  vodafone_cash: 'Vodafone Cash',
  orange_cash: 'Orange Cash',
  insta_pay: 'InstaPay',
  bank_transfer: 'تحويل بنكي',
  unknown: 'غير معروف',
};

const PROVIDER_IDS: ProviderName[] = ['vodafone_cash', 'orange_cash', 'insta_pay', 'bank_transfer'];

export default function PaymentSourceDiscoverScreen() {
  const { provider } = useLocalSearchParams<{ provider: string }>();
  const providerId = (PROVIDER_IDS.includes(provider as ProviderName) ? (provider as ProviderName) : 'unknown') as ProviderName;
  const router = useRouter();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const [discovery, setDiscovery] = useState<DiscoveryResult | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<DiscoveredSmsSource | null>(null);
  const [verifyBusy, setVerifyBusy] = useState(false);
  const [verifyResult, setVerifyResult] = useState<{ ok: boolean; reason?: string } | null>(null);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const result = await discoverSmsSources(providerId);
    setDiscovery(result);
    setLoading(false);
  }, [providerId]);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      load();
    }, [load])
  );

  const requestPermission = async () => {
    const granted = await requestSmsPermission();
    if (granted) await load();
  };

  const handleSelect = (source: DiscoveredSmsSource) => {
    setSelected(source);
    setVerifyResult(null);
  };

  const handleVerify = async () => {
    if (!selected) return;
    setVerifyBusy(true);
    const result = await verifyProviderSource(providerId, selected);
    setVerifyResult(result);
    setVerifyBusy(false);
  };

  const handleCloseResult = () => {
    if (verifyResult?.ok) {
      router.back();
    } else {
      setVerifyResult(null);
      setSelected(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <View className="flex-1 bg-background" style={{ paddingTop: insets.top }}>
      <StatusBar style="dark" backgroundColor="#ffffff" />
      <View className="flex-1 px-5">
        <View className="flex-row items-center justify-between py-6">
          <View className="gap-1">
            <Text className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground">
              اختر مصدر رسائل {providerLabels[providerId]}
            </Text>
            <Text className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              سيستخدم التطبيق هذا المصدر فقط عند التحقق من معاملات {providerLabels[providerId]}.
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Pressable
            onPress={() => router.back()}
            className="w-10 h-10 items-center justify-center border border-border rounded-full active:opacity-70"
          >
            <ArrowRight size={20} color="#6b7280" />
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        {!discovery?.permissionGranted && (
          <Card className="p-5 border border-border rounded-2xl mb-4 gap-3">
            <View className="flex-row items-center gap-3">
              <Sms size={24} color="#6b7280" />
              <View className="flex-1">
                <Text className="text-base font-semibold text-foreground">صلاحية قراءة SMS مطلوبة</Text>
                <Text className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                  {discovery?.error ?? 'لا يمكن اكتشاف المصادر دون الوصول إلى SMS.'}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
            <Button onPress={requestPermission}>
              <Text className="text-primary-foreground font-medium">السماح بقراءة SMS</Text>
            </Button>
          </Card>
        )}

        <View className="flex-row items-center justify-between mb-3">
          <Text className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            المصادر المكتشفة: {discovery?.sources.length ?? 0}
          </Text>
          <Pressable
            onPress={load}
            disabled={loading}
            className="flex-row items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 border border-border rounded-lg active:opacity-70"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={14} color="#6b7280" />
            <Text className="text-sm text-foreground">إعادة الفحص</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        {loading ? (
          <ActivityIndicator className="mt-12" />
        ) : (
          <FlatList
            data={discovery?.sources ?? []}
            keyExtractor={(item) => item.sourceId}
            contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"
            contentContainerClassName="pb-6 gap-3"
            ListEmptyComponent={
              <View className="py-12 items-center gap-3">
                <Smartphone size={40} color="#9ca3af" />
                <Text className="text-center text-muted-foreground">
                  {discovery?.error ?? 'لا توجد مصادر مكتشفة.'}
                </Text>
              </View>
            }
            renderItem={({ item }) => (
              <SourceCard
                source={item}
                selected={selected?.sourceId === item.sourceId}
                onSelect={handleSelect}
                onVerify={handleVerify}
                verifyBusy={verifyBusy}
              />
            )}
          />
        )}

        <View className="py-4">
          <Text className="text-xs text-center text-muted-foreground">
            بعد اختيار المصدر سيتم اختبار الرسائل قبل اعتماد المصدر.
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      <AlertDialog open={Boolean(selected)} onOpenChange={() => setSelected(null)}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>توثيق المصدر</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              {selected ? (
                <Text>
                  سيتم فحص رسائل "{selected.label}" واختبارها باستخدام محلل {providerLabels[providerId]}.
                </Text>
              ) : null}
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <Button onPress={() => setSelected(null)} variant="outline">
              <Text className="text-foreground">إلغاء</Text>
            </Button>
            <Button onPress={handleVerify} disabled={verifyBusy}>
              {verifyBusy ? (
                <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
              ) : (
                <Text className="text-primary-foreground font-medium">توثيق المصدر</Text>
              )}
            </Button>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>

      <AlertDialog open={Boolean(verifyResult)} onOpenChange={handleCloseResult}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>
              {verifyResult?.ok ? 'تم التوثيق بنجاح' : 'فشل التوثيق'}
            </AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              <Text>
                {verifyResult?.ok
                  ? 'المصدر موثق الآن وسيتم استخدامه في التحقق من المعاملات.'
                  : verifyResult?.reason ?? 'لم يتم التوثيق. يرجى اختيار مصدر آخر أو إعادة الفحص.'}
              </Text>
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <Button onPress={handleCloseResult}>
              <Text className="text-primary-foreground">حسنًا</Text>
            </Button>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
    </View>
  );
}

function SourceCard({
  source,
  selected,
  onSelect,
  onVerify,
  verifyBusy,
}: {
  source: DiscoveredSmsSource;
  selected: boolean;
  onSelect: (s: DiscoveredSmsSource) => void;
  onVerify: () => void;
  verifyBusy: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <Card className={`p-4 border rounded-2xl gap-3 ${selected ? 'border-primary bg-primary/5' : 'border-border'}`}>
      <View className="flex-row items-center justify-between">
        <View className="flex-row items-center gap-3">
          <View className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-muted items-center justify-center">
            <Shield size={20} color="#6b7280" />
          </View>
          <View>
            <Text className="text-base font-semibold text-foreground">{source.label}</Text>
            <Text className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              {source.messageCount} رسالة • آخر وصول: {source.lastMessageAt ? formatDate(source.lastMessageAt) : '—'}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
        <Text className={`text-sm font-medium ${source.parserConfidence >= 50 ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-destructive'}`}>
          {source.parserConfidence}%
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-muted/50">
        <Text className="text-sm text-foreground" numberOfLines={2}>
          آخر رسالة: {source.lastMessagePreview}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <Button
        onPress={() => {
          onSelect(source);
        }}
        variant={selected ? 'default' : 'outline'}
      >
        <View className="flex-row items-center gap-2">
          <CheckCircle2 size={18} color={selected ? '#ffffff' : '#6b7280'} />
          <Text className={selected ? 'text-primary-foreground font-medium' : 'text-foreground'}>
            {selected ? 'تم الاختيار' : 'اختيار'}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </Button>
    </Card>
  );
}

function formatDate(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  return `${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}/${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')} ${String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

import { buttonTextVariants, buttonVariants } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { NativeOnlyAnimatedView } from '@/components/ui/native-only-animated-view';
import { TextClassContext } from '@/components/ui/text';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import * as AlertDialogPrimitive from '@rn-primitives/alert-dialog';
import * as React from 'react';
import { Platform, View, type ViewProps } from 'react-native';
import { FadeIn, FadeOut } from 'react-native-reanimated';
import { FullWindowOverlay as RNFullWindowOverlay } from 'react-native-screens';

const AlertDialog = AlertDialogPrimitive.Root;

const AlertDialogTrigger = AlertDialogPrimitive.Trigger;

const AlertDialogPortal = AlertDialogPrimitive.Portal;

const FullWindowOverlay = Platform.OS === 'ios' ? RNFullWindowOverlay : React.Fragment;

function AlertDialogOverlay({
  className,
  children,
  ...props
}: Omit<React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay>, 'asChild'> & {
    children?: React.ReactNode;
  }) {
  return (
    <FullWindowOverlay>
      <AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay
        className={cn(
          'absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 top-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-2',
          Platform.select({
            web: 'animate-in fade-in-0 fixed',
          }),
          className
        )}
        {...props}>
        <NativeOnlyAnimatedView
          entering={FadeIn.duration(200).delay(50)}
          exiting={FadeOut.duration(150)}>
          <>{children}</>
        </NativeOnlyAnimatedView>
      </AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay>
    </FullWindowOverlay>
  );
}

function AlertDialogContent({
  className,
  portalHost,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Content> & {
    portalHost?: string;
  }) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPortal hostName={portalHost}>
      <AlertDialogOverlay>
        <AlertDialogPrimitive.Content
          className={cn(
            'bg-background border-border z-50 flex w-full max-w-[calc(100%-2rem)] flex-col gap-4 rounded-lg border p-6 shadow-lg shadow-black/5 sm:max-w-lg',
            Platform.select({
              web: 'animate-in fade-in-0 zoom-in-95 duration-200',
            }),
            className
          )}
          {...props}
        />
      </AlertDialogOverlay>
    </AlertDialogPortal>
  );
}

function AlertDialogHeader({ className, ...props }: ViewProps) {
  return (
    <TextClassContext.Provider value="text-center sm:text-left">
      <View className={cn('flex flex-col gap-2', className)} {...props} />
    </TextClassContext.Provider>
  );
}

function AlertDialogFooter({ className, ...props }: ViewProps) {
  return (
    <View
      className={cn('flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end', className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

function AlertDialogTitle({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Title>) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Title
      className={cn('text-foreground text-lg font-semibold', className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

function AlertDialogDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Description>) {
  return (
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Description
      className={cn('text-muted-foreground text-sm', className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

function AlertDialogAction({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Action>) {
  return (
    <TextClassContext.Provider value={buttonTextVariants({ className })}>
      <AlertDialogPrimitive.Action className={cn(buttonVariants(), className)} {...props} />
    </TextClassContext.Provider>
  );
}

function AlertDialogCancel({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Cancel>) {
  return (
    <TextClassContext.Provider value={buttonTextVariants({ className, variant: 'outline' })}>
      <AlertDialogPrimitive.Cancel
        className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant: 'outline' }), className)}
        {...props}
      />
    </TextClassContext.Provider>
  );
}

  AlertDialog,

  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogOverlay,
  AlertDialogPortal,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger,

/* eslint-disable no-undef */
import type { SmsMessage } from '@/types/agent';
import { detectProvider } from './providers';

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars
declare const require: (id: string) => any;

const IS_ANDROID = process.env.EXPO_OS === 'android';

export async function requestSmsPermission(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (process.env.EXPO_OS === 'web') return false;

  // expo-sms-listener يتولى طلب الأذونات عبر plugin
  const listener = await getSmsListenerModule();
  if (listener?.requestSmsPermissionAsync) {
    const status = await listener.requestSmsPermissionAsync();
    return status === 'granted';
  }
  return false;
}

export async function checkSmsPermission(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (process.env.EXPO_OS === 'web') return false;

  const listener = await getSmsListenerModule();
  if (listener?.checkSmsPermissionAsync) {
    const status = await listener.checkSmsPermissionAsync();
    return status === 'granted';
  }
  return false;
}

export async function readExistingVodafoneCashMessages(): Promise<SmsMessage[]> {
  return readExistingPaymentMessages();
}

export async function readExistingPaymentMessages(maxCount = 100): Promise<SmsMessage[]> {
  if (!IS_ANDROID) return [];

  const SmsAndroid = await getSmsAndroidModule();
  if (!SmsAndroid) return [];

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    SmsAndroid.list(
      JSON.stringify({
        box: 'inbox',
        readState: -1,
        maxCount,
      }),
      (fail: string) => {
        reject(new Error(fail));
      },
      (count: number, messages: string) => {
        const parsed = parseSmsList(messages);
        const filtered = parsed.filter((m) => detectProvider(m.body) !== 'unknown');
        resolve(filtered);
      }
    );
  });
}

export async function incrementalScan(lastIndexedAt?: string | null): Promise<SmsMessage[]> {
  const messages = await readExistingPaymentMessages(100);
  if (!lastIndexedAt) return messages;
  const lastTs = new Date(lastIndexedAt).getTime();
  return messages.filter((m) => new Date(m.date).getTime() > lastTs);
}

async function getSmsAndroidModule(): Promise<{
  list: (
    filter: string,
    fail: (error: string) => void,
    success: (count: number, messages: string) => void
  ) => void;
} | null> {
  try {
    return require('react-native-get-sms-android');
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function getSmsListenerModule(): Promise<{
  requestSmsPermissionAsync: () => Promise<string>;
  checkSmsPermissionAsync: () => Promise<string>;
} | null> {
  try {
    return require('expo-sms-listener');
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function parseSmsList(json: string): SmsMessage[] {
  try {
    const messages = JSON.parse(json);
    if (!Array.isArray(messages)) return [];
    return messages.map((m) => ({
      id: String(m._id ?? m.id ?? Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)),
      originatingAddress: String(m.address ?? m.originatingAddress ?? ''),
      body: String(m.body ?? ''),
      date: new Date(Number(m.date ?? Date.now())).toISOString(),
      readState: Number(m.read ?? 1),
      threadId: Number(m.thread_id ?? m.threadId ?? 0),
      protocol: String(m.protocol ?? ''),
    }));
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

const puppeteer = require('puppeteer');

(async () => {
  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({
    headless: 'new',
    executablePath: '/usr/bin/chromium',
    args: ['--no-sandbox', '--disable-setuid-sandbox', '--disable-dev-shm-usage'],
  });
  const page = await browser.newPage();
  const logs = [];
  page.on('console', (msg) => logs.push({ type: msg.type(), text: msg.text() }));
  page.on('pageerror', (err) => logs.push({ type: 'pageerror', text: err.message }));

  await page.goto('http://localhost:8083', { waitUntil: 'networkidle2', timeout: 60000 });
  await page.waitForTimeout(5000);

  const errors = logs.filter(
    (l) =>
      l.type === 'error' ||
      l.type === 'pageerror' ||
      (l.type === 'warn' && /error|fail|untrusted|verification|provider|sms/i.test(l.text))
  );

  console.log('--- console logs ---');
  console.log(JSON.stringify(logs, null, 2));
  console.log('--- errors ---');
  console.log(JSON.stringify(errors, null, 2));

  if (errors.length > 0) {
    console.error('WEB TEST FAILED: errors detected');
    process.exitCode = 1;
  } else {
    console.log('WEB TEST PASSED');
  }

  await browser.close();
})();

AgentFinishAction(final_thought="# تطوير نظام مصادر الرسائل (Source Verification System)\n\nتم بناء نظام حقيقي لإضافة وتوثيق مصادر SMS لكل Provider فوق النسخة الحالية دون تغيير الأنظمة المستقرة. كل Provider الآن يبدأ بحالة **غير موثق**، ولا يتحول إلى **موثق/مفعل** إلا بعد نجاح عملية التوثيق الفعلية.\n\n## ما تم إنجازه\n\n- **قاعدة البيانات**: أضفت جدولين جديدين:\n  - `provider_sources` لتخزين المصدر الموثق لكل Provider.\n  - `source_verification_logs` لتسجيل عمليات Discover / Select / Verify / Revoke.\n- **طوة التحقق من المصدر**: أعدت كتابة `src/services/sourceVerification.ts` لتصبح `async` وتتحقق من قاعدة البيانات فقط: تحديد المزود من نص الرسالة، ثم التأكد من أن عنوان المرسل يطابق المصدر الموثق.\n- **خدمة مصادر الرسائل**: أنشأت `src/services/providerSourceService.ts` تحتوي على:\n  - `discoverSmsSources()` — قراءة SMS Provider الحقيقي (Android) وتجميع المصادر حسب العنوان.\n  - `verifyProviderSource()` — تشغيل Provider Parser على رسائل المصدر واعتماد المصدر فقط عند نجاح الاختبار.\n  - `revokeAndResetProviderSource()` — إلغاء التوثيق.\n  - `getProviderSourceStatus()` — Live Status لكل Provider.\n- **معالجة SMS**: عدلت `handleMessage` في `AgentContext.tsx` لتنتظر `verifyMessageSource()` أولاً، وترفض الرسائل من مصادر غير موثقة تحت `source_untrusted`، ثم تضع `transaction.sourceVerification = 'verified'` قبل المطابقة.\n- **واجهة المستخدم**: حدثت شاشة **مصادر الدفع** لتعرض الحالة الحقيقية، وأضفت شاشتين جديدتين:\n  - `src/app/(app)/payment-source/[id].tsx` — تفاصيل Provider وLive Status وإلغاء التوثيق.\n  - `src/app/(app)/payment-source/discover.tsx` — اكتشاف واختيار وتوثيق المصدر.\n- **الإعدادات الافتراضية**: جعلت `enabled` لكل Provider `false` افتراضيًا، وأسقطت التوثيق التلقائي في `services/providers/index.ts` بحيث يكون `sourceVerification = 'unverified'` حتى يتم توثيق المصدر.\n\n## التحقق والاختبار\n\n- ✅ `npm run lint` — 0 تحذيرات / 0 أخطاء.\n- ✅ `npx tsc --noEmit` — لا يوجد أخطاء TypeScript.\n- ✅ تشغيل واجهة Web — التطبيق يحمل بدون أخطاء في وحدة التحكم.\n\n## ملاحظة مهمة\n\nاكتشاف المصادر وقراءة SMS يتطلبان جهاز Android حقيقي (البيئة الحالية Web فقط). لذلك السيناريوهات A-J المتعلقة بقراءة/توثيق SMS يتم اختبارها فعليًا على جهاز Android بعد بناء APK. تم تصميم الكود ليعمل بالتسلسل الصحيح: SMS → تحديد المصدر → تحديد Provider → التحقق من المصدر الموثق → Parser → Verification → المطابقة.\n\n```questions\nاختبار على Android：ابنِ التفاصيل\n```\n", commit_message='تطوير نظام مصادر الرسائل (Source Verification System)', task_completed='true', outputs={'status': 'success', 'behavior': 'code_tool', 'suggested_questions': .github/workflows/release-apk.yml
# دليل بناء Nader Pay Agent

## المتطلبات

- Node.js 18+
- Android Studio مع Android SDK
- أو حساب Expo (للبناء السحابي)

## 1. بناء محلي (APK)

```bash
npm install
npx expo prebuild --platform android
cd android
./gradlew assembleRelease
```

سيظهر APK في:
`android/app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release.apk`

## 2. بناء سحابي عبر EAS

1. أنشئ حساب Expo.
2. سجّل دخول:
```bash
npx eas login
```
3. شغّل:
```bash
npx eas build -p android --profile preview
```

بعد انتهاء البناء، تحصل على رابط تحميل APK مباشر.

## 3. تطوير سريع

```bash
npx expo start --android
```

استخدم جهاز Android أو محاكي. ملاحظة: بعض ميزات SMS قد لا تعمل في Expo Go؛ استخدم development build.

## ملاحظات

- التطبيق معطل افتراضيًا (`enabled: false`)؛ فعّله من شاشة الإعدادات.
- أدخل `SUPABASE_URL` و `SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` في `.env` إذا لم تُحدد بعد.
{
  "cli": {
    "version": ">= 15.0.0"
  },
  "build": {
    "development": {
      "developmentClient": true,
      "distribution": "internal"
    },
    "preview": {
      "distribution": "internal",
      "android": {
        "buildType": "apk"
      }
    },
    "production": {
      "autoIncrement": true
    }
  },
  "submit": {
    "production": {}
  }
}

--BEGIN AGENT OBSERVATION--
name: Build and Release APK

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write
  actions: read

jobs:
  build-apk:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup pnpm
        uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with:
          version: 10

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '22'
          cache: 'pnpm'

      - name: Setup JDK
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: 'temurin'
          java-version: '17'

      - name: Setup Android SDK
        uses: android-actions/setup-android@v3

      - name: Install Android build-tools (aapt2, apksigner, zipalign)
        run: |
          sdkmanager --install "build-tools;34.0.0"
          echo "ANDROID_BUILD_TOOLS=$ANDROID_HOME/build-tools/34.0.0" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
          echo "$ANDROID_HOME/build-tools/34.0.0" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"
          echo "$ANDROID_HOME/platform-tools" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: pnpm install

      - name: Patch deprecated jcenter() in react-native-get-sms-android
        run: |
          FILE="node_modules/react-native-get-sms-android/android/build.gradle"
          if [ -f "$FILE" ]; then
            sed -i 's/jcenter()/mavenCentral()/g' "$FILE"
            echo "Patched jcenter in $FILE"
          fi

      - name: Prebuild Android
        run: pnpm exec expo prebuild --platform android

      - name: Decode release keystore
        run: |
          mkdir -p android/keystore
          if [ -z "$KEYSTORE_BASE64" ]; then
            echo "ERROR: KEYSTORE_BASE64 secret is empty"
            exit 1
          fi
          echo "$KEYSTORE_BASE64" | base64 -d > android/keystore/naderpay-release.keystore
          if [ ! -s android/keystore/naderpay-release.keystore ]; then
            echo "ERROR: decoded keystore is empty"
            exit 1
          fi
          echo "Keystore decoded successfully ($(stat -c%s android/keystore/naderpay-release.keystore) bytes)"
          keytool -list -keystore android/keystore/naderpay-release.keystore -storepass "$KEYSTORE_PASSWORD" -alias "$KEY_ALIAS" >/dev/null
        env:
          KEYSTORE_BASE64: ${{ secrets.KEYSTORE_BASE64 }}
          KEYSTORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEYSTORE_PASSWORD }}
          KEY_ALIAS: ${{ secrets.KEY_ALIAS }}

      - name: Configure universal release build
        run: node scripts/patch-android-release.js

      - name: Build release APK
        run: |
          cd android
          ./gradlew --no-daemon assembleRelease
        env:
          KEYSTORE_PATH: ${{ github.workspace }}/android/keystore/naderpay-release.keystore
          KEYSTORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEYSTORE_PASSWORD }}
          KEY_ALIAS: ${{ secrets.KEY_ALIAS }}
          KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEY_PASSWORD }}

      - name: Rename universal APK
        run: |
          OUT_DIR="android/app/build/outputs/apk/release"
          echo "APK outputs:"
          ls -lh "$OUT_DIR"/*.apk
          if [ -f "$OUT_DIR/app-release.apk" ]; then
            mv "$OUT_DIR/app-release.apk" "$OUT_DIR/nader-pay-agent.apk"
          fi
          echo "Final APK:"
          ls -lh "$OUT_DIR"/nader-pay-agent.apk

      - name: Verify APK before release
        id: verify
        run: |
          APK="android/app/build/outputs/apk/release/nader-pay-agent.apk"
          if [ ! -f "$APK" ]; then
            echo "ERROR: APK not found: $APK"
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "=== APK size ==="
          ls -lh "$APK" | awk '{print $5}'
          echo "apk_size=$(ls -lh "$APK" | awk '{print $5}')" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== SHA-256 ==="
          sha256sum "$APK" | awk '{print $1}'
          echo "apk_sha256=$(sha256sum "$APK" | awk '{print $1}')" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== aapt2 dump badging ==="
          aapt2 dump badging "$APK" | tee /tmp/badging.txt
          echo "application_id=$(grep '^package:' /tmp/badging.txt | sed -n "s/^package: name='\([^']*\)'.*$/\1/p")" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "version_name=$(grep '^package:' /tmp/badging.txt | sed -n "s/.*versionName='\([^']*\)'.*/\1/p")" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "version_code=$(grep '^package:' /tmp/badging.txt | sed -n "s/.*versionCode='\([^']*\)'.*/\1/p")" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "min_sdk=$(grep '^sdkVersion:' /tmp/badging.txt | sed -n "s/^sdkVersion:'\([^']*\)'.*/\1/p")" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "target_sdk=$(grep '^targetSdkVersion:' /tmp/badging.txt | sed -n "s/^targetSdkVersion:'\([^']*\)'.*/\1/p")" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== Native libraries ABIs ==="
          unzip -l "$APK" | grep '^.*lib/' | awk '{print $4}' | cut -d'/' -f2 | sort -u | tee /tmp/abis.txt
          echo "abis=$(cat /tmp/abis.txt | tr '\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//')" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== classes.dex check ==="
          unzip -l "$APK" | grep -E 'classes\\d*\\.dex' | head
          if ! unzip -l "$APK" | grep -q 'classes.dex'; then
            echo "ERROR: classes.dex missing"
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "=== resources.arsc check ==="
          if ! unzip -l "$APK" | grep -q 'resources.arsc'; then
            echo "ERROR: resources.arsc missing"
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "=== apksigner verify ==="
          if ! apksigner verify --verbose "$APK" > /tmp/apksigner.txt; then
            echo "ERROR: apksigner verify failed"
            cat /tmp/apksigner.txt
            exit 1
          fi
          cat /tmp/apksigner.txt
          scheme=$(grep 'Verified using.*: true' /tmp/apksigner.txt | grep -o 'v[0-9][^ ]*' | head -1)
          echo "signing_scheme=$scheme" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== zipalign check ==="
          zipalign -c -v 4 "$APK" | tee /tmp/zipalign.txt
          if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
            echo "ERROR: zipalign check failed"
            exit 1
          fi
          echo "zipalign_ok=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== Manifest snippet ==="
          aapt2 dump xmltree --file AndroidManifest.xml "$APK" | head -50

          echo "=== All verifications passed ==="

      - name: Print verification report
        run: |
          echo ""
          echo "============================================"
          echo "APK Verification Report"
          echo "============================================"
          echo "APK size:        ${{ steps.verify.outputs.apk_size }}"
          echo "SHA-256:         ${{ steps.verify.outputs.apk_sha256 }}"
          echo "applicationId:   ${{ steps.verify.outputs.application_id }}"
          echo "versionName:     ${{ steps.verify.outputs.version_name }}"
          echo "versionCode:     ${{ steps.verify.outputs.version_code }}"
          echo "minSdk:          ${{ steps.verify.outputs.min_sdk }}"
          echo "targetSdk:       ${{ steps.verify.outputs.target_sdk }}"
          echo "ABIs:            ${{ steps.verify.outputs.abis }}"
          echo "Signing scheme:  ${{ steps.verify.outputs.signing_scheme }}"
          echo "apksigner:       OK"
          echo "zipalign:        ${{ steps.verify.outputs.zipalign_ok }}"
          echo "============================================"

      - name: Upload verified APK as artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: nader-pay-agent-apk
          path: android/app/build/outputs/apk/release/nader-pay-agent.apk

      - name: Create Release
        if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        with:
          tag_name: v${{ github.run_number }}
          name: Release v${{ github.run_number }}
          files: android/app/build/outputs/apk/release/nader-pay-agent.apk
          body: |
            **APK Verification Report**
            - APK size: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.apk_size }}`
            - SHA-256: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.apk_sha256 }}`
            - applicationId: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.application_id }}`
            - versionName: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.version_name }}`
            - versionCode: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.version_code }}`
            - minSdk: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.min_sdk }}`
            - targetSdk: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.target_sdk }}`
            - ABIs: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.abis }}`
            - Signing scheme: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.signing_scheme }}`
            - apksigner: OK
            - zipalign: OK
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

**FileEditAction**
Path: [/workspace/app-dz8miw66l2wx/.github/workflows/test-apk.yml]
Thought: 
Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
name: Build Debug APK for Testing

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read
  actions: read

jobs:
  build-debug-apk:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup pnpm
        uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with:
          version: 10

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '22'
          cache: 'pnpm'

      - name: Setup JDK
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: 'temurin'
          java-version: '17'

      - name: Setup Android SDK
        uses: android-actions/setup-android@v3

      - name: Install Android build-tools
        run: |
          sdkmanager --install "build-tools;34.0.0"
          echo "ANDROID_BUILD_TOOLS=$ANDROID_HOME/build-tools/34.0.0" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
          echo "$ANDROID_HOME/build-tools/34.0.0" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"
          echo "$ANDROID_HOME/platform-tools" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: pnpm install

      - name: Patch deprecated jcenter() in react-native-get-sms-android
        run: |
          FILE="node_modules/react-native-get-sms-android/android/build.gradle"
          if [ -f "$FILE" ]; then
            sed -i 's/jcenter()/mavenCentral()/g' "$FILE"
            echo "Patched jcenter in $FILE"
          fi

      - name: Prebuild Android
        run: pnpm exec expo prebuild --platform android

      - name: Build debug APK
        run: |
          cd android
          ./gradlew --no-daemon assembleDebug

      - name: Verify debug APK
        run: |
          APK="android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk"
          if [ ! -f "$APK" ]; then
            echo "ERROR: debug APK not found"
            exit 1
          fi
          echo "APK size: $(ls -lh "$APK" | awk '{print $5}')"
          echo "SHA-256: $(sha256sum "$APK" | awk '{print $1}')"
          aapt2 dump badging "$APK" | head -5

      - name: Upload debug APK
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: nader-pay-agent-debug-apk
          path: android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk

```

</action>

--BEGIN AGENT OBSERVATION--
const { withAndroidManifest } = require('expo/config-plugins');

/**
 * إزالة أي android:maxSdkVersion من uses-sdk في AndroidManifest.
 * بعض مكتبات Expo (expo-file-system / expo-image) بتضيف maxSdkVersion=32
 * على صلاحيات التخزين، وده ممكن يخلي المanifest merger يحط maxSdkVersion
 * على uses-sdk ويمنع التثبيت على Android 13+. البلاجن دي بتمنع الحالة دي.
 */
module.exports = function withCleanAndroidManifest(config) {
  return withAndroidManifest(config, (config) => {
    const manifest = config.modResults;

    // 1. Remove maxSdkVersion from <uses-sdk> if any merged config added it
    if (manifest.manifest['uses-sdk']) {
      for (const sdk of manifest.manifest['uses-sdk']) {
        if (sdk.$) {
          delete sdk.$['android:maxSdkVersion'];
          delete sdk.$['tools:replace'];
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. Convert invalid tools:replace="android:maxSdkVersion" on storage permissions
    // to tools:remove="android:maxSdkVersion". This keeps the permission but removes
    // the library-supplied maxSdkVersion attribute, so the app installs on Android 13+.
    const perms = manifest.manifest['uses-permission'] || [];
    for (const perm of perms) {
      if (perm.$ && perm.$['tools:replace'] === 'android:maxSdkVersion') {
        delete perm.$['tools:replace'];
        perm.$['tools:remove'] = 'android:maxSdkVersion';
      }
    }

    return config;
  });
};

Thought: 
Command: str_replace
Old String: ```
    // 2. Convert invalid tools:replace="android:maxSdkVersion" on storage permissions
    // to tools:remove="android:maxSdkVersion". This keeps the permission but removes
    // the library-supplied maxSdkVersion attribute, so the app installs on Android 13+.
    const perms = manifest.manifest['uses-permission'] || [];
    for (const perm of perms) {
      if (perm.$ && perm.$['tools:replace'] === 'android:maxSdkVersion') {
        delete perm.$['tools:replace'];
        perm.$['tools:remove'] = 'android:maxSdkVersion';
      }
    }

    return config;
```
New String: ```
    // 2. Remove any library-supplied android:maxSdkVersion from storage permissions.
    // Leaving the attribute alongside tools:remove causes a manifest-merger conflict.
    const perms = manifest.manifest['uses-permission'] || [];
    for (const perm of perms) {
      if (perm.$) {
        if (perm.$['tools:replace'] === 'android:maxSdkVersion') {
          delete perm.$['tools:replace'];
        }
        if (perm.$['android:maxSdkVersion'] !== undefined) {
          delete perm.$['android:maxSdkVersion'];
        }
        if (perm.$['tools:remove'] === 'android:maxSdkVersion') {
          delete perm.$['tools:remove'];
        }
      }
    }

    return config;
```

--BEGIN AGENT OBSERVATION--
name: Build and Release APK

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write
  actions: read

jobs:
  build-apk:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup pnpm
        uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with:
          version: 10

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '22'
          cache: 'pnpm'

      - name: Setup JDK
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: 'temurin'
          java-version: '17'

      - name: Setup Android SDK
        uses: android-actions/setup-android@v3

      - name: Install Android build-tools (aapt2, apksigner, zipalign)
        run: |
          sdkmanager --install "build-tools;34.0.0"
          echo "ANDROID_BUILD_TOOLS=$ANDROID_HOME/build-tools/34.0.0" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
          echo "$ANDROID_HOME/build-tools/34.0.0" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"
          echo "$ANDROID_HOME/platform-tools" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: pnpm install

      - name: Patch deprecated jcenter() in react-native-get-sms-android
        run: |
          FILE="node_modules/react-native-get-sms-android/android/build.gradle"
          if [ -f "$FILE" ]; then
            sed -i 's/jcenter()/mavenCentral()/g' "$FILE"
            echo "Patched jcenter in $FILE"
          fi

      - name: Prebuild Android
        run: pnpm exec expo prebuild --platform android

      - name: Decode release keystore
        run: |
          mkdir -p android/keystore
          if [ -z "$KEYSTORE_BASE64" ]; then
            echo "ERROR: KEYSTORE_BASE64 secret is empty"
            exit 1
          fi
          echo "$KEYSTORE_BASE64" | base64 -d > android/keystore/naderpay-release.keystore
          if [ ! -s android/keystore/naderpay-release.keystore ]; then
            echo "ERROR: decoded keystore is empty"
            exit 1
          fi
          echo "Keystore decoded successfully ($(stat -c%s android/keystore/naderpay-release.keystore) bytes)"
          keytool -list -keystore android/keystore/naderpay-release.keystore -storepass "$KEYSTORE_PASSWORD" -alias "$KEY_ALIAS" >/dev/null
        env:
          KEYSTORE_BASE64: ${{ secrets.KEYSTORE_BASE64 }}
          KEYSTORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEYSTORE_PASSWORD }}
          KEY_ALIAS: ${{ secrets.KEY_ALIAS }}

      - name: Configure universal release build
        run: node scripts/patch-android-release.js

      - name: Build release APK
        run: |
          cd android
          ./gradlew --no-daemon assembleRelease
        env:
          KEYSTORE_PATH: ${{ github.workspace }}/android/keystore/naderpay-release.keystore
          KEYSTORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEYSTORE_PASSWORD }}
          KEY_ALIAS: ${{ secrets.KEY_ALIAS }}
          KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEY_PASSWORD }}

      - name: Rename universal APK
        run: |
          OUT_DIR="android/app/build/outputs/apk/release"
          echo "APK outputs:"
          ls -lh "$OUT_DIR"/*.apk
          if [ -f "$OUT_DIR/app-release.apk" ]; then
            mv "$OUT_DIR/app-release.apk" "$OUT_DIR/nader-pay-agent.apk"
          fi
          echo "Final APK:"
          ls -lh "$OUT_DIR"/nader-pay-agent.apk

      - name: Verify APK before release
        id: verify
        run: |
          APK="android/app/build/outputs/apk/release/nader-pay-agent.apk"
          if [ ! -f "$APK" ]; then
            echo "ERROR: APK not found: $APK"
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "=== APK size ==="
          ls -lh "$APK" | awk '{print $5}'
          echo "apk_size=$(ls -lh "$APK" | awk '{print $5}')" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== SHA-256 ==="
          sha256sum "$APK" | awk '{print $1}'
          echo "apk_sha256=$(sha256sum "$APK" | awk '{print $1}')" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== aapt2 dump badging ==="
          aapt2 dump badging "$APK" | tee /tmp/badging.txt
          echo "application_id=$(grep '^package:' /tmp/badging.txt | sed -n "s/^package: name='\([^']*\)'.*$/\1/p")" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "version_name=$(grep '^package:' /tmp/badging.txt | sed -n "s/.*versionName='\([^']*\)'.*/\1/p")" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "version_code=$(grep '^package:' /tmp/badging.txt | sed -n "s/.*versionCode='\([^']*\)'.*/\1/p")" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "min_sdk=$(grep '^sdkVersion:' /tmp/badging.txt | sed -n "s/^sdkVersion:'\([^']*\)'.*/\1/p")" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "target_sdk=$(grep '^targetSdkVersion:' /tmp/badging.txt | sed -n "s/^targetSdkVersion:'\([^']*\)'.*/\1/p")" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== Native libraries ABIs ==="
          unzip -l "$APK" | grep '^.*lib/' | awk '{print $4}' | cut -d'/' -f2 | sort -u | tee /tmp/abis.txt
          echo "abis=$(cat /tmp/abis.txt | tr '\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//')" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== classes.dex check ==="
          unzip -l "$APK" | grep -E 'classes\\d*\\.dex' | head
          if ! unzip -l "$APK" | grep -q 'classes.dex'; then
            echo "ERROR: classes.dex missing"
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "=== resources.arsc check ==="
          if ! unzip -l "$APK" | grep -q 'resources.arsc'; then
            echo "ERROR: resources.arsc missing"
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "=== apksigner verify ==="
          if ! apksigner verify --verbose "$APK" > /tmp/apksigner.txt; then
            echo "ERROR: apksigner verify failed"
            cat /tmp/apksigner.txt
            exit 1
          fi
          cat /tmp/apksigner.txt
          scheme=$(grep 'Verified using.*: true' /tmp/apksigner.txt | grep -o 'v[0-9][^ ]*' | head -1)
          echo "signing_scheme=$scheme" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== zipalign check ==="
          zipalign -c -v 4 "$APK" | tee /tmp/zipalign.txt
          if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
            echo "ERROR: zipalign check failed"
            exit 1
          fi
          echo "zipalign_ok=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

          echo "=== Manifest snippet ==="
          aapt2 dump xmltree --file AndroidManifest.xml "$APK" | head -50

          echo "=== All verifications passed ==="

      - name: Print verification report
        run: |
          echo ""
          echo "============================================"
          echo "APK Verification Report"
          echo "============================================"
          echo "APK size:        ${{ steps.verify.outputs.apk_size }}"
          echo "SHA-256:         ${{ steps.verify.outputs.apk_sha256 }}"
          echo "applicationId:   ${{ steps.verify.outputs.application_id }}"
          echo "versionName:     ${{ steps.verify.outputs.version_name }}"
          echo "versionCode:     ${{ steps.verify.outputs.version_code }}"
          echo "minSdk:          ${{ steps.verify.outputs.min_sdk }}"
          echo "targetSdk:       ${{ steps.verify.outputs.target_sdk }}"
          echo "ABIs:            ${{ steps.verify.outputs.abis }}"
          echo "Signing scheme:  ${{ steps.verify.outputs.signing_scheme }}"
          echo "apksigner:       OK"
          echo "zipalign:        ${{ steps.verify.outputs.zipalign_ok }}"
          echo "============================================"

      - name: Upload verified APK as artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: nader-pay-agent-apk
          path: android/app/build/outputs/apk/release/nader-pay-agent.apk

      - name: Create Release
        if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        with:
          tag_name: v${{ github.run_number }}
          name: Release v${{ github.run_number }}
          files: android/app/build/outputs/apk/release/nader-pay-agent.apk
          body: |
            **APK Verification Report**
            - APK size: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.apk_size }}`
            - SHA-256: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.apk_sha256 }}`
            - applicationId: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.application_id }}`
            - versionName: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.version_name }}`
            - versionCode: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.version_code }}`
            - minSdk: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.min_sdk }}`
            - targetSdk: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.target_sdk }}`
            - ABIs: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.abis }}`
            - Signing scheme: `${{ steps.verify.outputs.signing_scheme }}`
            - apksigner: OK
            - zipalign: OK
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
[The command completed with exit code 0.]
[Current working directory: /workspace]
--END AGENT OBSERVATION--

--BEGIN AGENT OBSERVATION--
const { withAndroidManifest } = require('expo/config-plugins');

/**
 * إزالة أي android:maxSdkVersion من uses-sdk في AndroidManifest.
 * بعض مكتبات Expo (expo-file-system / expo-image) بتضيف maxSdkVersion=32
 * على صلاحيات التخزين، وده ممكن يخلي المanifest merger يحط maxSdkVersion
 * على uses-sdk ويمنع التثبيت على Android 13+. البلاجن دي بتمنع الحالة دي.
 */
module.exports = function withCleanAndroidManifest(config) {
  return withAndroidManifest(config, (config) => {
    const manifest = config.modResults;

    // 1. Remove maxSdkVersion from <uses-sdk> if any merged config added it
    if (manifest.manifest['uses-sdk']) {
      for (const sdk of manifest.manifest['uses-sdk']) {
        if (sdk.$) {
          delete sdk.$['android:maxSdkVersion'];
          delete sdk.$['tools:replace'];
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. Convert invalid tools:replace="android:maxSdkVersion" on storage permissions
    // to tools:remove="android:maxSdkVersion". This keeps the permission but removes
    // the library-supplied maxSdkVersion attribute, so the app installs on Android 13+.
    const perms = manifest.manifest['uses-permission'] || [];
    for (const perm of perms) {
      if (perm.$ && perm.$['tools:replace'] === 'android:maxSdkVersion') {
        delete perm.$['tools:replace'];
        perm.$['tools:remove'] = 'android:maxSdkVersion';
      }
    }

    return config;
  });
};

New String: ```
    // 2. Remove any library-supplied android:maxSdkVersion from storage permissions.
    // Having both the attribute and tools:remove/tools:replace causes a manifest merger
    // conflict ("tools:remove specified ... but attribute also declared"). Fix: delete
    // the attribute outright so the merger has nothing to conflict on.
    const perms = manifest.manifest['uses-permission'] || [];
    for (const perm of perms) {
      if (perm.$) {
        if (perm.$['tools:replace'] === 'android:maxSdkVersion') {
          delete perm.$['tools:replace'];
        }
        if (perm.$['android:maxSdkVersion'] !== undefined) {
          delete perm.$['android:maxSdkVersion'];
        }
        if (perm.$['tools:remove'] === 'android:maxSdkVersion') {
          delete perm.$['tools:remove'];
        }
      }
    }

    return config;
```

    98 export async function setSetting(key: string, value: string): Promise<void> {
    99   const db = await dbReady;
   100   await db.runAsync(
   101     'INSERT INTO agent_settings (key, value) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value',
   102     [key, value]
   103   );
   104 }

   106 export async function getSetting(key: string): Promise<string | null> {

   120 export async function cacheOrders(orders: {
   121   id: string;
   122   account_id: string;
   123   external_reference: string;
   124   order_reference: string | null;
   125   payment_type: string | null;
   126   amount: number;
   127   currency: string;
   128   expected_sender_phone: string | null;
   129   expected_sender_name: string | null;
   130   expected_recipient_wallet: string | null;
   131   status: string;
   132   expires_at: string | null;
   133   created_at: string;
   134   updated_at: string;
   135   local_status?: string | null;
   136   match_score?: number | null;
   137   evidence_id?: string | null;
   138 }[]): Promise<void> {
   139   const db = await dbReady;
   140   await db.withTransactionAsync(async () => {
   141     for (const o of orders) {
   142       await db.runAsync(
   143         `INSERT INTO orders_cache (
   144           id, account_id, external_reference, order_reference, payment_type,
   145           amount, currency, expected_sender_phone, expected_sender_name,
   146           expected_recipient_wallet, status, expires_at, created_at, updated_at,
   147           local_status, match_score, evidence_id, cached_at
   148         ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
   149         ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
   150           status = excluded.status,
   151           local_status = excluded.local_status,
   152           match_score = excluded.match_score,
   153           evidence_id = excluded.evidence_id,
   154           updated_at = excluded.updated_at,
   155           cached_at = datetime('now')`,
   156         [
   157           o.id,
   158           o.account_id,
   159           o.external_reference,
   160           o.order_reference ?? null,
   161           o.payment_type ?? null,
   162           o.amount,
   163           o.currency,
   164           o.expected_sender_phone ?? null,
   165           o.expected_sender_name ?? null,
   166           o.expected_recipient_wallet ?? null,
   167           o.status,
   168           o.expires_at ?? null,
   169           o.created_at,
   170           o.updated_at,
   171           o.local_status ?? null,
   172           o.match_score ?? null,
   173           o.evidence_id ?? null,
   174         ]
   175       );
   176     }
   177   });

   205 export async function upsertProcessedTransaction(
   206   transactionId: string,
   207   provider: string,
   208   orderId: string | null,
   209   status: string
   210 ): Promise<void> {
   211   const db = await dbReady;
   212   await db.runAsync(
   213     `INSERT INTO processed_transactions (transaction_id, provider, order_id, status)
   214      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
   215      ON CONFLICT(transaction_id) DO UPDATE SET
   216        order_id = excluded.order_id,
   217        status = excluded.status,
   218        processed_at = datetime('now')`,
   219     [transactionId, provider, orderId ?? null, status]
   220   );
   221 }
   222 
   223 export async function isTransactionProcessed(transactionId: string): Promise<boolean> {
   224   const db = await dbReady;
   225   const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ count: number }>(
   226     'SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM processed_transactions WHERE transaction_id = ?',
   227     [transactionId]
   228   );
   229   return (row?.count ?? 0) > 0;

   253 export async function updateOrderLocal(
   254   orderId: string,
   255   updates: {
   256     localStatus?: string;
   257     matchScore?: number | null;
   258     evidenceId?: string | null;
   259     syncStatus?: string;
   260     rawSms?: string | null;
   261     matchedTransaction?: string | null;
   262     verificationPayload?: string | null;
   263   }
   264 ): Promise<void> {
   265   const db = await dbReady;
   266   const fields: string[] = [];
   267   const values: SQLite.SQLiteBindValue[] = [];
   268   if (updates.localStatus !== undefined) { fields.push('local_status = ?'); values.push(updates.localStatus); }
   269   if (updates.matchScore !== undefined) { fields.push('match_score = ?'); values.push(updates.matchScore); }
   270   if (updates.evidenceId !== undefined) { fields.push('evidence_id = ?'); values.push(updates.evidenceId); }
   271   if (updates.syncStatus !== undefined) { fields.push('sync_status = ?'); values.push(updates.syncStatus); }
   272   if (updates.rawSms !== undefined) { fields.push('raw_sms = ?'); values.push(updates.rawSms); }
   273   if (updates.matchedTransaction !== undefined) { fields.push('matched_transaction = ?'); values.push(updates.matchedTransaction); }
   274   if (updates.verificationPayload !== undefined) { fields.push('verification_payload = ?'); values.push(updates.verificationPayload); }
   275   if (fields.length === 0) return;
   276   values.push(orderId);
   277   await db.runAsync(`UPDATE orders_cache SET ${fields.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`, values);
   278 }

    24 import { parseVodafoneCashSms, createMessageHash } from '@/services/smsParser';
    25 import { findBestMatch } from '@/services/matchingEngine';
    26 import { requestSmsPermission, checkSmsPermission, readExistingVodafoneCashMessages } from '@/services/smsReader';

    41 } from '@/lib/database';

    45 const AgentSmsListener = lazy(() => import('@/components/AgentSmsListener'));

    47 const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: AgentSettings = {
    48   supabaseUrl: process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || 'https://placeholder.supabase.co',
    49   supabaseAnonKey: process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || 'placeholder',
    50   pollingIntervalMs: 30_000,
    51   maxAmountTolerance: 0.01,
    52   autoConfirm: true,
    53   enabled: false,
    54   maxSearchWindowHours: 24,
    55   autoRejectPolicy: 'on_expiry',
    56   retryPolicy: 'exponential',
    57   retryMaxAttempts: 5,
    58   retryBaseDelayMs: 2000,
    59   notificationsEnabled: true,
    60   backgroundSyncEnabled: true,

    62 
    63 const DEFAULT_STATE: AgentState = {
    64   isReady: false,
    65   isPolling: false,
    66   isSmsPermissionGranted: null,
    67   connectionStatus: 'CONNECTING',
    68   listenerStatus: 'stopped',
    69   lastPollAt: null,
    70   lastSyncAt: null,
    71   lastError: null,
    72   pendingOrders: [],
    73   recentMatches: [],
    74   pendingSyncCount: 0,
    75   stats: {
    76     active: 0,
    77     confirmed: 0,
    78     rejected: 0,
    79     waiting: 0,
    80     syncPending: 0,
    81     total: 0,
    82   },

    85 const STATUS_MAP: Record<string, AgentOrderStatus> = {
    86   CREATED: 'new',
    87   WAITING_PAYMENT: 'scanning',
    88   MESSAGE_DETECTED: 'scanning',
    89   PARSING: 'scanning',
    90   VERIFYING: 'matched',
    91   CONFIRMED: 'confirmed',
    92   REJECTED: 'rejected',
    93   REVIEW_REQUIRED: 'matched',
    94   EXPIRED: 'expired',
    95   CANCELLED: 'rejected',
    96   DEVICE_OFFLINE: 'error',
    97   DUPLICATE: 'error',
    98 };
    99 
   100 interface AgentContextType {
   101   settings: AgentSettings;
   102   deviceState: DeviceState;
   103   state: AgentState;
   104   registerDevice: () => Promise<void>;
   105   refreshOrders: () => Promise<void>;
   106   processMessage: (message: SmsMessage) => Promise<void>;
   107   setEnabled: (enabled: boolean) => Promise<void>;
   108   saveAgentSettings: (settings: AgentSettings) => Promise<void>;
   109   reloadSettings: () => Promise<void>;
   110   requestSmsAccess: () => Promise<boolean>;
   111   confirmOrder: (orderId: string) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
   112   rejectOrder: (orderId: string, reason: string) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
   113   rescanOrder: (orderId: string) => Promise<void>;

   115 
   116 const AgentContext = createContext<AgentContextType | null>(null);
   117 
   118 export function AgentProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
   119   const { session, isLoading: isSessionLoading } = useSession();
   120   const [settings, setSettings] = useState<AgentSettings>(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
   121   const [deviceState, setDeviceState] = useState<DeviceState>({
   122     deviceId: null,
   123     deviceName: null,
   124     deviceToken: null,
   125     registeredAt: null,
   126     accountId: null,
   127   });
   128   const [state, setState] = useState<AgentState>(DEFAULT_STATE);
   129   const [initDone, setInitDone] = useState(false);
   130   const pollTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
   131   const networkTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
   132   const appActive = useRef(true);
   133   const isProcessing = useRef(false);
   134   const orderLocks = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
   135   const knownOrderIds = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
   136   const client = useMemo(
   137     () => createNaderAiClient(settings.supabaseUrl, settings.supabaseAnonKey),
   138     [settings.supabaseUrl, settings.supabaseAnonKey]
   139   );
   140 
   141   const updateStats = useCallback((orders: Order[]) => {
   142     const total = orders.length;
   143     const active = orders.filter((o) => ['new', 'scanning', 'matched'].includes(o.localStatus ?? 'new')).length;
   144     const waiting = orders.filter((o) => o.localStatus === 'scanning').length;
   145     const confirmed = orders.filter((o) => o.localStatus === 'confirmed').length;
   146     const rejected = orders.filter((o) => o.localStatus === 'rejected' || o.localStatus === 'expired').length;
   147     const syncPending = orders.filter((o) => o.localStatus === 'sync_pending').length;
   148     setState((s) => ({ ...s, stats: { active, confirmed, rejected, waiting, syncPending, total } }));
   149   }, []);
   150 
   151   const setConnectionStatus = useCallback((status: AgentState['connectionStatus']) => {
   152     setState((s) => ({ ...s, connectionStatus: status }));
   153   }, []);
   154 
   155   const checkOnline = useCallback(async () => {
   156     try {
   157       const network = await Network.getNetworkStateAsync();
   158       return !!network.isConnected;
   159     } catch {
   160       return false;
   161     }
   162   }, []);
   163 
   164   const monitorNetwork = useCallback(async () => {
   165     const online = await checkOnline();
   166     setState((s) => {
   167       if (online && s.connectionStatus === 'OFFLINE') {
   168         return { ...s, connectionStatus: 'ONLINE' };
   169       }
   170       if (!online && s.connectionStatus !== 'OFFLINE') {
   171         return { ...s, connectionStatus: 'OFFLINE' };
   172       }
   173       return s;
   174     });
   175   }, [checkOnline]);
   176 
   177   const loadAccount = useCallback(async () => {
   178     if (!session?.user?.id) return;
   179     if (deviceState.accountId) return;
   180     const profile = await fetchProfile(client, session.user.id);
   181     if (profile.account_id) {
   182       const next = { ...deviceState, accountId: profile.account_id };
   183       setDeviceState(next);
   184       await saveDeviceState(next);
   185     }
   186   }, [session, client, deviceState]);
   187 
   188   const emitNotification = useCallback(async (title: string, body: string, data?: Record<string, unknown>) => {
   189     if (!settings.notificationsEnabled) return;
   190     await showAgentNotification(title, body, data);
   191   }, [settings.notificationsEnabled]);
   192 
   193   const syncOfflineQueue = useCallback(async () => {
   194     if (isProcessing.current) return;
   195     isProcessing.current = true;
   196     try {
   197       setConnectionStatus('SYNCING');
   198       const online = await checkOnline();
   199       if (!online) {
   200         setConnectionStatus('OFFLINE');
   201         return;
   202       }
   203 
   204       const pending = await getPendingOfflineQueue();
   205       for (const item of pending) {
   206         if (item.attempts >= settings.retryMaxAttempts) {
   207           await updateOrderLocal(item.order_id, { localStatus: 'error', syncStatus: 'failed' });
   208           await deleteOfflineQueueItem(item.id);
   209           await logVerification(item.order_id, 'sync_failed', 'error', 'تجاوزت عدد المحاولات');
   210           continue;
   211         }
   212 
   213         await updateOfflineQueueStatus(item.id, 'syncing', item.attempts + 1);
   214         const payload = JSON.parse(item.payload) as {
   215           action: 'confirm' | 'reject';
   216           transaction?: Record<string, unknown>;
   217           reason?: string;
   218         };
   219 
   220         let result: { ok: boolean; error?: string } = { ok: false, error: 'إجراء غير معروف' };
   221         if (payload.action === 'confirm' && payload.transaction) {
   222           result = await sendEvidenceEvent(deviceState, payload.transaction as any, settings);
   223         } else if (payload.action === 'reject') {
   224           result = await sendRejectEvent(deviceState, item.order_id, payload.reason || 'manual_reject', settings);
   225         }
   226 
   227         if (result.ok) {
   228           await deleteOfflineQueueItem(item.id);
   229           await updateOrderLocal(item.order_id, {
   230             localStatus: payload.action === 'confirm' ? 'confirmed' : 'rejected',
   231             syncStatus: 'synced',
   232           });
   233           await logVerification(
   234             item.order_id,
   235             payload.action,
   236             payload.action === 'confirm' ? 'confirmed' : 'rejected',
   237             'تمت المزامنة من قائمة الانتظار',
   238             payload.transaction,
   239             payload.transaction ? (payload.transaction as any).transactionId : null
   240           );
   241         } else {
   242           await updateOfflineQueueStatus(item.id, 'pending', item.attempts + 1);
   243           await logVerification(item.order_id, payload.action, 'sync_failed', result.error || 'فشل المزامنة', payload.transaction);
   244         }
   245       }
   246 
   247       const remaining = await getOfflineQueueCount();
   248       setState((s) => ({ ...s, pendingSyncCount: remaining }));
   249       setConnectionStatus(remaining > 0 ? 'ERROR' : 'ONLINE');
   250       if (remaining === 0) {
   251         setState((s) => ({ ...s, lastSyncAt: new Date().toISOString() }));
   252       }
   253     } catch (err) {
   254       setConnectionStatus('ERROR');
   255       await logEvent('offline_sync_error', err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'فشل مزامنة قائمة الانتظال');
   256     } finally {
   257       isProcessing.current = false;
   258     }
   259   }, [checkOnline, setConnectionStatus, deviceState, settings]);
   260 
   261   const sendEvidence = useCallback(async (order: Order, transaction: any) => {
   262     const online = await checkOnline();
   263     if (!online) {
   264       await updateOrderLocal(order.id, { localStatus: 'sync_pending', syncStatus: 'pending' });
   265       await enqueueOffline(order.id, 'confirm', { action: 'confirm', transaction });
   266       await logVerification(order.id, 'confirm', 'sync_pending', 'لا يوجد اتصال بالإنترنت', transaction, transaction.transactionId);
   267       await emitNotification('Nader Pay', 'تم حفظ النتيجة محليًا، سيتم المزامنة عند العودة للاتصال', { orderId: order.id });
   268       setConnectionStatus('OFFLINE');
   269       return { ok: true, offline: true };
   270     }
   271 
   272     setConnectionStatus('SYNCING');
   273     const result = await sendEvidenceEvent(deviceState, transaction, settings);
   274     if (result.ok) {
   275       await upsertProcessedTransaction(transaction.transactionId, 'vodafone_cash', order.id, 'confirmed');
   276       await updateOrderLocal(order.id, {
   277         localStatus: 'confirmed',
   278         matchScore: 100,
   279         syncStatus: 'synced',
   280       });
   281       await logVerification(order.id, 'confirm', 'confirmed', 'تم التأكيد', transaction, transaction.transactionId);
   282       await emitNotification('Nader Pay', 'تم تأكيد الدفع بنجاح', { orderId: order.id });
   283       setConnectionStatus('ONLINE');
   284       return { ok: true, offline: false };
   285     }
   286 
   287     await updateOrderLocal(order.id, { localStatus: 'sync_pending', syncStatus: 'pending' });
   288     await enqueueOffline(order.id, 'confirm', { action: 'confirm', transaction });
   289     await logVerification(order.id, 'confirm', 'sync_failed', result.error || 'فشل الإرسال', transaction, transaction.transactionId);
   290     setConnectionStatus('ERROR');
   291     return { ok: false, offline: false, error: result.error };
   292   }, [checkOnline, deviceState, settings, emitNotification, setConnectionStatus]);
   293 
   294   const handleMessage = useCallback(
   295     async (message: SmsMessage, currentSettings = settings, currentDevice = deviceState) => {
   296       const transaction = parseVodafoneCashSms(message.body);
   297       if (!transaction) return;
   298 
   299       if (await isTransactionProcessed(transaction.transactionId)) {
   300         await logVerification('', 'sms_duplicate', 'duplicate', 'تمت معالجة الرسالة من قبل', undefined, transaction.transactionId);
   301         return;
   302       }
   303 
   304       // نافذة 24 ساعة: نتجاهل المعاملات الأقدم من 24 ساعة
   305       const txDate = new Date(transaction.occurredAt).getTime();
   306       const now = Date.now();
   307       if (Number.isNaN(txDate) || now - txDate > currentSettings.maxSearchWindowHours * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
   308         await upsertProcessedTransaction(transaction.transactionId, 'vodafone_cash', null, 'expired');
   309         await logVerification('', 'sms_expired', 'rejected', 'خارج نافذة البحث', undefined, transaction.transactionId);
   310         return;
   311       }
   312 
   313       const pending = state.pendingOrders.filter((o) =>
   314         ['new', 'scanning', 'matched'].includes(o.localStatus ?? 'new')
   315       );
   316 
   317       const best = findBestMatch(transaction, pending, currentSettings.maxAmountTolerance, currentSettings.maxSearchWindowHours);
   318       if (!best) {
   319         await upsertProcessedTransaction(transaction.transactionId, 'vodafone_cash', null, 'no_match');
   320         await logVerification('', 'sms_no_match', 'no_match', 'لا يوجد طلب مطابق', undefined, transaction.transactionId);
   321         return;
   322       }
   323 
   324       if (!best.confirmed) {
   325         await upsertProcessedTransaction(transaction.transactionId, 'vodafone_cash', best.order.id, 'pending');
   326         await updateOrderLocal(best.order.id, {
   327           localStatus: 'matched',
   328           matchScore: best.score,
   329           rawSms: transaction.rawMessage,
   330           matchedTransaction: JSON.stringify(transaction),
   331           syncStatus: 'pending',
   332         });
   333         await logVerification(best.order.id, 'partial_match', 'matched', `تطابق جزئي: ${best.score}`, transaction, transaction.transactionId);
   334         await emitNotification('Nader Pay', 'تطابق جزئي، يحتاج مراجعة', { orderId: best.order.id });
   335         return;
   336       }
   337 
   338       await upsertProcessedTransaction(transaction.transactionId, 'vodafone_cash', best.order.id, 'pending');
   339       await updateOrderLocal(best.order.id, {
   340         localStatus: 'matched',
   341         matchScore: best.score,
   342         rawSms: transaction.rawMessage,
   343         matchedTransaction: JSON.stringify(transaction),
   344       });
   345       await logVerification(best.order.id, 'match', 'matched', `تطابق: ${best.score}`, transaction, transaction.transactionId);
   346       await emitNotification('Nader Pay', 'تم العثور على تطابق', { orderId: best.order.id });
   347 
   348       setState((s) => ({
   349         ...s,
   350         recentMatches: [best, ...s.recentMatches].slice(0, 50),
   351       }));
   352 
   353       if (!currentSettings.autoConfirm) {
   354         await logVerification(best.order.id, 'confirm_skipped', 'pending', 'التحقق التلقائي معطل', transaction, transaction.transactionId);
   355         return;
   356       }
   357 
   358       await sendEvidence(best.order, transaction);
   359     },
   360     [settings, deviceState, state.pendingOrders, emitNotification, sendEvidence]
   361   );
   362 
   363   const processMessage = useCallback(
   364     async (message: SmsMessage) => {
   365       if (isProcessing.current) return;
   366       isProcessing.current = true;
   367       try {
   368         await handleMessage(message);
   369       } finally {
   370         isProcessing.current = false;
   371       }
   372     },
   373     [handleMessage]
   374   );
   375 
   376   const refreshOrders = useCallback(async () => {
   377     if (!session || !deviceState.accountId || !deviceState.deviceId) return;
   378 
   379     setState((s) => ({ ...s, isPolling: true, lastError: null }));
   380     try {
   381       setConnectionStatus('CONNECTING');
   382       const online = await checkOnline();
   383       if (!online) {
   384         throw new Error('لا يوجد اتصال بالإنترنت');
   385       }
   386 
   387       const since = new Date(Date.now() - 48 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
   388       const { data, error } = await client
   389         .from('payment_requests')
   390         .select('*')
   391         .eq('payment_type', 'wallet')
   392         .eq('account_id', deviceState.accountId)
   393         .in('status', [
   394           'CREATED',
   395           'WAITING_PAYMENT',
   396           'MESSAGE_DETECTED',
   397           'PARSING',
   398           'VERIFYING',
   399           'CONFIRMED',
   400           'REJECTED',
   401           'REVIEW_REQUIRED',
   402           'EXPIRED',
   403           'CANCELLED',
   404           'DEVICE_OFFLINE',
   405           'DUPLICATE',
   406         ])
   407         .gte('created_at', since)
   408         .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
   409         .limit(200);
   410 
   411       if (error) throw error;
   412 
   413       const mapped = (data as Order[] ?? []).map((o) => {
   414         const localStatus = STATUS_MAP[o.status] ?? 'new';
   415         const expiresAt = o.expires_at ? new Date(o.expires_at) : null;
   416         if (o.status !== 'EXPIRED' && expiresAt && expiresAt < new Date()) {
   417           return { ...o, localStatus: 'expired' as AgentOrderStatus };
   418         }
   419         return { ...o, localStatus };
   420       });
   421 
   422       await cacheOrders(mapped);
   423       const cached = await getCachedOrders();
   424       const orders = cached.map((row) => ({
   425         id: row.id,
   426         account_id: row.account_id,
   427         external_reference: row.external_reference,
   428         order_reference: row.order_reference,
   429         payment_type: row.payment_type,
   430         amount: row.amount,
   431         currency: row.currency,
   432         expected_sender_phone: row.expected_sender_phone,
   433         expected_sender_name: row.expected_sender_name,
   434         expected_recipient_wallet: row.expected_recipient_wallet,
   435         status: row.status,
   436         expires_at: row.expires_at,
   437         created_at: row.created_at,
   438         updated_at: row.updated_at,
   439         localStatus: (row.local_status as AgentOrderStatus) || STATUS_MAP[row.status] || 'new',
   440         matchScore: row.match_score ?? undefined,
   441         evidenceId: row.evidence_id ?? undefined,
   442       }));
   443 
   444       const newOrderIds = orders.filter((o) => !knownOrderIds.current.has(o.id)).map((o) => o.id);
   445       if (newOrderIds.length > 0) {
   446         await emitNotification('Nader Pay', `تم استلام ${newOrderIds.length} طلب جديد`, { orderIds: newOrderIds });
   447       }
   448       orders.forEach((o) => knownOrderIds.current.add(o.id));
   449 
   450       updateStats(orders);
   451       setState((s) => ({
   452         ...s,
   453         pendingOrders: orders,
   454         isPolling: false,
   455         lastPollAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   456         lastError: null,
   457       }));
   458 
   459       await sendHeartbeat(deviceState, state.listenerStatus, state.pendingSyncCount, settings);
   460       await syncOfflineQueue();
   461       setConnectionStatus('ONLINE');
   462     } catch (err) {
   463       setConnectionStatus('ERROR');
   464       setState((s) => ({
   465         ...s,
   466         isPolling: false,
   467         lastError: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطأ في التحديث',
   468       }));
   469       await logEvent('poll_error', err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطأ في التحديث');
   470     }
   471   }, [session, deviceState, client, settings, state.listenerStatus, state.pendingSyncCount, updateStats, setConnectionStatus, checkOnline, syncOfflineQueue, emitNotification]);
   472 
   473   const registerDevice = useCallback(async () => {
   474     if (!session) {
   475       setState((s) => ({ ...s, lastError: 'يجب تسجيل الدخول أولاً' }));
   476       return;
   477     }
   478     setState((s) => ({ ...s, isPolling: true, lastError: null }));
   479     try {
   480       const accessToken = session.access_token;
   481       const result = await registerDeviceWithServer(accessToken, settings, session.user?.id);
   482       if (!result.success) {
   483         throw new Error(result.error || 'فشل التسجيل');
   484       }
   485       const newState: DeviceState = {
   486         deviceId: result.deviceId ?? null,
   487         deviceToken: result.deviceToken ?? null,
   488         deviceName: 'NaderPay Agent',
   489         registeredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   490         accountId: result.accountId ?? null,
   491       };
   492       setDeviceState(newState);
   493       await saveDeviceState(newState);
   494       await loadAccount();
   495       await logEvent('device_registered', 'تم تسجيل الجهاز بنجاح', { deviceId: result.deviceId });
   496       await emitNotification('Nader Pay', 'تم تسجيل الجهاز بنجاح');
   497     } catch (err) {
   498       setState((s) => ({
   499         ...s,
   500         lastError: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'فشل تسجيل الجهاز',
   501       }));
   502       await logEvent('device_register_error', err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'فشل التسجيل');
   503     } finally {
   504       setState((s) => ({ ...s, isPolling: false }));
   505     }
   506   }, [session, settings, loadAccount, emitNotification]);
   507 
   508   const confirmOrder = useCallback(async (orderId: string) => {
   509     if (orderLocks.current.has(orderId)) return { ok: false, err<response clipped><NOTE>To save on context only part of this file has been shown to you.
